The **MTV Video Music Awards**, commonly abbreviated as **VMAs**, is an award show presented by [MTV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV) to honor the best in the [music video](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Music_video) medium. The ceremony has often been called the [Super Bowl](27718.md) for youth, an acknowledgment of the VMA ceremony's ability to draw millions of youth from teens to 20-somethings each year.[^cite_note-nyt1-1] By 2001, the VMA had become a coveted award.[^cite_note-bbc1-2]

The annual VMA ceremony occurs before the end of summer and held either in late August or mid-September, and broadcast live on CBS. The [first VMA ceremony](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1984_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) was held in 1984 at [New York City](645042.md)'s [Radio City Music Hall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radio_City_Music_Hall). The ceremonies are normally held in either New York City or [Los Angeles](18110.md). However, the ceremonies have also been hosted in [Miami](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miami,_Florida), [Las Vegas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Las_Vegas_Valley), [Newark, New Jersey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Newark,_New_Jersey), and more recently, in [Elmont, New York](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elmont,_New_York), on [Long Island](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Long_Island).[^cite_note-3]

The statue given to winners is an astronaut on the Moon, one of the earliest representations of MTV, and was colloquially called a "moonman", though it has been called a "moon person" by MTV since the [2017 ceremony](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017_MTV_Video_Music_Awards). The statue was conceived by [Manhattan Design](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manhattan_Design), who were also designers of the original MTV logo, based on the network's debut [network identification](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Station_identification) animation utilizing [Apollo 11](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apollo_11) mission footage, created by [Fred Seibert](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fred_Seibert) and produced by [Alan Goodman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alan_Goodman) and [Buzz Potamkin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buzz_Potamkin) at [Buzzco Associates](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buzzco_Associates). The statue is now made by [Society Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Society_Awards), a New York City-based firm.[^cite_note-4] Since the [2006 ceremony](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), viewers are able to vote for their favorite videos in all general categories.[^cite_note-5]

[Taylor Swift](5422144.md) is the most awarded artist in the history of the VMAs, having won 33 trophies between 2009 and 2026,[^cite_note-6] which includes a record-breaking six Video of the Year wins ("[Bad Blood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bad_Blood_\(Taylor_Swift_song\))", "[You Need to Calm Down](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/You_Need_to_Calm_Down)", *[All Too Well: The Short Film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/All_Too_Well:_The_Short_Film)*, "[Anti-Hero](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Hero_\(song\))", "[Fortnight](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fortnight_\(song\))", and "[The Fate of Ophelia](81126617.md)").[^cite_note-7] On September 21, 2026, Swift was announced as the inaugural recipient of the Artist Director Honors, recognizing her work in music video direction.[^cite_note-8]

## Notable moments

### 1980s



[Radio City Music Hall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radio_City_Music_Hall), site of 12 VMAs, including the [first](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1984_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)

**1984**: at the [first MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1984_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) in [1984](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1984_in_music), [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) performed her hit "[Like a Virgin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Like_a_Virgin_\(song\))" wearing a combination [bustier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bustier)/[wedding gown](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wedding_gown), including her trademark "Boy Toy" belt. During the performance, she rolled around on the floor, revealing lace stockings and a [garter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Garter_\(stockings\)).[^cite_note-birthicon-9] [Cyndi Lauper](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cyndi_Lauper) spoke in "*[Exorcist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Exorcist)*-esque gibberish" to explain the VMA rules right before winning the Best Female video for "[Girls Just Want to Have Fun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Girls_Just_Want_to_Have_Fun)".[^cite_note-cindi-10] [David Bowie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Bowie), [the Beatles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Beatles) and director [Richard Lester](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richard_Lester) were rewarded with the first ever [Video Vanguard Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_Video_Vanguard_Award) for their work in pioneering the [music video](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Music_video). The Cars' "[You Might Think](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/You_Might_Think)" won the very first video of the year, beating out [Michael Jackson](14995351.md)'s ["Thriller"](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thriller_\(song\)) and [Herbie Hancock](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Herbie_Hancock)'s "Rockit".[^cite_note-11]

**1987**: At the [1987 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1987_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Peter Gabriel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Gabriel) won ten awards, including the [Video Vanguard Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Video_Vanguard_Award) and [Video of the Year](476903.md) for his video "[Sledgehammer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sledgehammer_\(Peter_Gabriel_song\))", holding the VMA record for most Moonmen in a single night.

**1988**: At the [1988 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1988_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Michael Jackson](14995351.md) appeared for the first time. A pre-recorded live performance of [*Bad*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bad_\(album\)) was shown."[^cite_note-elton-12] He was also awarded the [Video Vanguard Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV_Video_Music_Award_%E2%80%93_Video_Vanguard),[^cite_note-mjvanguard-13] which was later renamed in his honor.

**1989**: Comedian [Andrew Dice Clay](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrew_Dice_Clay) appeared at the *[1989 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1989_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)* to promote his new movie, *[The Adventures of Ford Fairlane](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Adventures_of_Ford_Fairlane)*, earning a "lifetime ban" from the network when he introduced [Cher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cher) with some of his recently notorious [nursery rhymes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nursery_rhyme) that contained vulgar language and references.[^cite_note-andrewdiceclay-14] After performing with [Tom Petty](61853.md), [Guns N' Roses](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guns_N'_Roses) guitarist [Izzy Stradlin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Izzy_Stradlin) was assaulted by [Mötley Crüe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/M%C3%B6tley_Cr%C3%BCe) [lead singer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lead_singer) [Vince Neil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vince_Neil), leading to a verbal battle between Neil and [Guns N' Roses](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guns_N'_Roses) lead singer [Axl Rose](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Axl_Rose).[^cite_note-pettyassault-15]

[Jon Bon Jovi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jon_Bon_Jovi) and [Richie Sambora](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richie_Sambora) turned out a stripped-down acoustic performance of the [Bon Jovi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bon_Jovi) hits "[Livin' on a Prayer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Livin'_on_a_Prayer)" and "[Wanted Dead or Alive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wanted_Dead_or_Alive_\(Bon_Jovi_song\))", and in the process possibly provided the inspirational spark for [MTV Unplugged](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV_Unplugged).[^cite_note-vma1989-16][^cite_note-foo-17] While the [showrunners](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Showrunners) have stated that it was already in development by the time of the event, they've also credited the pair's performance with influencing the show to go from initially being meant only for "young, up-and-coming artists" into being a simplified showcase for the "big, stadium, electric-arena-type acts".[^cite_note-18]

[Paula Abdul](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paula_Abdul) was nominated for six awards, picking up four wins. She performed a seven-minute medley of her singles "[Straight Up](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Straight_Up_\(Paula_Abdul_song\))", "[Cold Hearted](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cold_Hearted_\(Paula_Abdul_song\))", and "[Forever Your Girl](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forever_Your_Girl_\(song\))".[^cite_note-vma1989-16]

When [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) won the Viewer's Choice Award (sponsored by Pepsi-Cola) for her "[Like a Prayer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Like_a_Prayer_\(song\))" video, she thanked [Pepsi-Cola](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pepsi-Cola_Company) in her acceptance speech "for causing so much controversy". Pepsi-Cola had paid Madonna \$5 million to appear in a commercial that would predominantly feature the world premiere of "Like a Prayer"; the commercial, titled "Make a Wish", depicted Madonna drinking Pepsi and watching a home video of her eighth birthday. The tone that the commercial sought to convey sharply contrasted with the music video. When Pepsi executives saw the video, they yanked the advertisement after only two airings, in an attempt to dissociate themselves from Madonna.[^cite_note-19] She also gave one of the most memorable performances of her hit "[Express Yourself](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Express_Yourself_\(Madonna_song\))", as a preview of what would become her [Blond Ambition World Tour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blond_Ambition_World_Tour).[^cite_note-vma1989-16]

### 1990s



[Nirvana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nirvana_\(band\)) performing at the [1992 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1992_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)

**1990**: at the *[1990 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1990_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)*, [Madonna](142056.md) performed her single "[Vogue](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vogue_\(Madonna_song\))", which featured Madonna and her backup dancers dressed in an 18th-century French theme, with Madonna bearing great resemblance to [Marie Antoinette](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marie_Antoinette). The performance consisted of both a dramatic historical reinterpretation of "Vogue" as well as her dramatically becorseted breasts.[^cite_note-madonna1990-20]

**1991**: during the award show the MTV Video Vanguard Award was renamed to the [Michael Jackson Video Vanguard Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_Video_Vanguard_Award) from then on, in honor of his contributions to the culture of music videos[^cite_note-21] by changing them from a promotional tool featuring musicians playing instruments and singing, to a "short film" with a storyline.[^cite_note-22]

A conflict between [Poison](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Poison_\(band\))'s [Bret Michaels](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bret_Michaels) and [C.C. DeVille](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/C.C._DeVille) culminated in a fistfight at the *[1991 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1991_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)*. DeVille was fired and replaced by [Pennsylvanian](23332.md) guitarist [Richie Kotzen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richie_Kotzen).[^cite_note-vma1991-23] [Paul Reubens](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Reubens) had his first public appearance, during the opening montage, following an arrest for lewd-conduct earlier that year. Taking the stage in costume as [Pee-wee Herman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pee-wee_Herman), he received a standing ovation, after which he asked the audience, "Heard any good jokes lately?".[^cite_note-peewee-24] After his appearance, [Van Halen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Van_Halen) made their television debut, performing "[Poundcake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Poundcake_\(song\))". [Metallica](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metallica) was another highlight of the performances with "[Enter Sandman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Enter_Sandman)".

[Prince](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prince_\(musician\)) and [The New Power Generation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Power_Generation) performed their song "[Gett Off](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gett_Off)" on a [Caligula](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Caligula)-esque set, with Prince dressed in a yellow mesh outfit which exposed his buttocks. His trousers were parodied numerous times throughout the following year, on the sketch comedy TV series *[In Living Color](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/In_Living_Color)*, and even at the following year's VMAs by radio [shock jock](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shock_jock) [Howard Stern](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Howard_Stern). It also marked the final TV appearance of [Kiss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kiss_\(band\)) with [Eric Carr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eric_Carr) prior to Carr's death in November 1991.

**1992**: in the [1992 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1992_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), MTV requested [Nirvana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nirvana_\(band\)) perform "[Smells Like Teen Spirit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Smells_Like_Teen_Spirit)", while the band itself had indicated it preferred to play new songs "[Rape Me](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rape_Me)" and "[tourette's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/In_Utero_\(album\))". Network executives continued to push for "Teen Spirit" but finally offered the band a choice to play either "Teen Spirit" or "[Lithium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lithium_\(Nirvana_song\))", which the band appeared to accept. At the performance, Nirvana began to play, and [Kurt Cobain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kurt_Cobain) played the first few chords of the song, "Rape Me", much to the horror of MTV execs, before continuing their regular performance of "Lithium". Near the end of the song, frustrated that his amplifier had stopped functioning, bassist [Krist Novoselic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Krist_Novoselic) decided to toss his bass into the air for dramatic effect. He misjudged the landing, and the bass ended up bouncing off of his forehead, forcing him to stumble off the stage in a daze.[^cite_note-25]

Backstage and before the show, [Guns N' Roses](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guns_N'_Roses) vocalist [Axl Rose](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Axl_Rose) challenged Cobain to a fight after he, his wife, [Hole](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hole_\(band\)) lead singer [Courtney Love](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Courtney_Love), and Nirvana bandmates [Krist Novoselic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Krist_Novoselic) and [Dave Grohl](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dave_Grohl) egged him on. At the end of Nirvana's performance, while Cobain was trashing the band's equipment, Dave Grohl ran to the microphone and shouted "Hi, Axl! Where's Axl?" repeatedly. Guns N' Roses' video for the [ballad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ballad) "[November Rain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/November_Rain)" won the MTV Video Music Award for best cinematography. During the show, the band performed "November Rain" with singer [Elton John](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elton_John). Because of the dispute Rose had with Cobain, moments before the "November Rain" performance, Cobain spat on the keys of what he thought was Axl's piano. Cobain later revealed that he was shocked to see Elton John play on the piano he had spat on.[^cite_note-elton-12] During the commercial break, the Alien 3 Pepsi commercial was shown.

Radio host [Howard Stern](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Howard_Stern) appeared as [Fartman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fartman_\(Howard_Stern\)), Stern's radio superhero, wearing a buttocks-exposing costume obviously inspired by Prince's outfit from the previous year. Stern was a presenter for best hard rock/metal performance with actor [Luke Perry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luke_Perry) (after several other celebrities turned him down).

**1993**: at the [1993 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1993_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) opened the show in a gender-bending performance of her song "[Bye Bye Baby](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bye_Bye_Baby_\(Madonna_song\))", in which Madonna and her two backup singers, dressed in tuxedos and top hats, danced with women in corsets in a choreographed, highly sexual routine.[^cite_note-vma1993-26]

[RuPaul](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RuPaul) and [Milton Berle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Milton_Berle), who had conflicts backstage, presented an award together. When Berle touched RuPaul's breasts, RuPaul ad-libbed the line "So you used to wear gowns, but now you're wearing diapers."[^cite_note-vma1993-26]

Rapper [Snoop Dogg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snoop_Dogg) presented the Best R\&B Video award with [Dr. Dre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dr._Dre) and [George Clinton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Clinton_\(funk_musician\)). At the time, Snoop was wanted in connection with the week-old drive-by murder of an L.A. gang member.[^cite_note-vma1993-26]

[Janet Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Janet_Jackson) closed the show with her performance of "[That's the Way Love Goes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/That's_the_Way_Love_Goes_\(Janet_Jackson_song\))" & "[If](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/If_\(Janet_Jackson_song\))".

**1994**: at the [1994 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1994_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) on September 8, months after [a profanity-laced appearance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_on_Late_Show_with_David_Letterman) on the *[Late Show with David Letterman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Late_Show_with_David_Letterman)*, [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) was announced to present the award for Best Video of the Year. She came out, arm-in-arm with an unannounced [David Letterman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Letterman), to a wild ovation. At the microphone, Letterman told her "I'll be in the car. Watch your language", and left.[^cite_note-letterman-27]

Recently betrothed couple [Michael Jackson](14995351.md) and [Lisa Marie Presley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lisa_Marie_Presley) received a standing ovation as they walked on stage hand-in-hand. After turning to the audience and proclaiming, "And just think, nobody thought this would last", Jackson grabbed Presley and kissed her.[^cite_note-vma1994-28][^cite_note-29]

**1995**: at the [1995 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1995_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Hole](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hole_\(band\)) perform the song "[Violet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Violet_\(Hole_song\))" from their major-label debut album *[Live Through This](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Live_Through_This)*. This was one of the first major televised performances given by frontwoman [Courtney Love](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Courtney_Love) following [the death](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_of_Kurt_Cobain) of her husband [Kurt Cobain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kurt_Cobain) and the death of her band's bassist [Kristen Pfaff](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kristen_Pfaff) in 1994. Before beginning the song, Love dedicated the performance to her husband and different people in the entertainment industry who had recently died: "This is for Kurt, and Kristen, and [River](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/River_Phoenix), and [Joe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murder_of_Joe_Cole), and today Joni Abbott, this is for you." Abbott worked in the Talent Relations department at MTV and had recently committed suicide.[^cite_note-30] The song ended with Love throwing her guitar, knocking the microphone stand into the crowd and pushing over speaker-boxes with bandmate [Eric Erlandson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eric_Erlandson) before exiting the stage.[^cite_note-arizona1-31] Love also caused a stir when she interrupted a post-ceremony interview with [Kurt Loder](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kurt_Loder) and [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) by throwing her make-up compacts at the singer as they broadcast outside the awards venue.[^cite_note-32]

[Michael Jackson](14995351.md) performed for over 15 minutes to a medley of his main songs, including "[Scream](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scream_\(Michael_Jackson_and_Janet_Jackson_song\))", and danced his signature moves, including the robot, moonwalk and the relatively unknown "[Bankhead Bounce](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bankhead_Bounce)". While Slash accompanied Jackson and played guitar on "[Black or White](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_or_White) and the beginning of Billie Jean".[^cite_note-vma1995-33] This performance was voted by the public as the Best VMA Pop Performance and Most Iconic VMA Performance in 2011 with more than half the votes.[^cite_note-34][^cite_note-35]

[TLC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TLC_\(band\)) was the big winner of the night won four awards, including "Viewer's Choice", "Best Group Video", and "Video of the Year" (Waterfalls).

**1996**: at the [1996 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1996_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), the four original members of [Van Halen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Van_Halen) received 20-second [standing ovation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Standing_ovation) when they made their first public appearance together since their break-up in [1985](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1985_in_music).

Several weeks later, the public learned that Van Halen would not reunite with Roth. Roth released a statement apologizing to fans, stating that he was an unwitting participant in a publicity stunt to sell more copies of the greatest hits album, *[Best Of Vol. 1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Best_Of_%E2%80%93_Volume_I_\(Van_Halen_album\))*, and that he had been led to believe that he was rejoining Van Halen. The following day, Eddie and [Alex Van Halen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alex_Van_Halen) released a statement, stating that they had been honest with Roth, and never led him to believe that he had been re-hired.

During British band [Oasis](161515.md)' performance at the show, lead vocalist [Liam Gallagher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liam_Gallagher) made rude gestures at brother [Noel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Noel_Gallagher) as he was playing his guitar solo, then spat beer all over the stage before storming off.[^cite_note-vma1996-36]

[Alanis Morissette](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alanis_Morissette) performed "Your House", a [hidden track](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hidden_track) from her bestseller album "[Jagged Little Pill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jagged_Little_Pill)".

The recently reunited [Kiss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kiss_\(band\)) closed the show with a special concert aired from the [Brooklyn Bridge](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brooklyn_Bridge).[^cite_note-arizona2-37]

[Tupac Shakur](31341.md) made his final public appearance before [his murder](38900595.md).

**1997**: at the [1997 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1997_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Pat Smear](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pat_Smear) announced that he was leaving [Foo Fighters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foo_Fighters) halfway through their performance and presented his replacement, [Franz Stahl](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franz_Stahl), who had been a member of the band [Scream](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scream_\(band\)) with [Dave Grohl](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dave_Grohl).[^cite_note-foofighters-38]

The [Spice Girls](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spice_Girls), who won the best dance video award for their music video "[Wannabe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wannabe_\(song\))", wore a black strap on their left arms as a sign of grief because of [Diana, Princess of Wales](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diana,_Princess_of_Wales)'s [death](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Diana,_Princess_of_Wales) four days prior to the event.

While accepting the MTV Video Music Award for Best New Artist Video that year for "[Sleep to Dream](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sleep_to_Dream)", [Fiona Apple](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fiona_Apple) appealed to her audience not to be enamored of [celebrity culture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Celebrity_culture). She proclaimed, "this world is bullshit" and quoted [Maya Angelou](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maya_Angelou), saying "go with yourself". Though her comments were generally greeted with cheers and applause at the awards ceremony, the media backlash was huge. Some considered her remarks to be hypocritical, however she was unapologetic: "When I have something to say, I'll say it."[^cite_note-vma1997-39][^cite_note-cracked-40][^cite_note-fiona-41]

[Marilyn Manson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marilyn_Manson) performed the song "[The Beautiful People](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Beautiful_People_\(song\))", as the grand finale, and the video for this song was nominated for "Best Rock Video" and "Best Special Effects", marking one of the most significant performances for the band.[^cite_note-manson-42]

**1998**: at the [1998 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1998_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), during the original broadcast of the show, a commercial faded in the [Nine Inch Nails](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nine_Inch_Nails) NIN logo on a black screen while playing a combination of music that started as a solo piano piece and morphed into an electronic/industrial beat (which would later found out to be the songs "La Mer" and "[Into the Void](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Into_the_Void_\(Nine_Inch_Nails_song\))", which share many melodic components and can be considered variations on a theme) and ended with [Trent Reznor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trent_Reznor) screaming "Tried to save myself, but myself kept slipping away" and the word "ninetynine" in the trademark NIN reversed-N font. This was only shown once during the original broadcast, was edited out of all repeats.

[Geri Halliwell](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geri_Halliwell) attended the event, one of her first public appearances since she left the [Spice Girls](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spice_Girls).[^cite_note-43]

At the red carpet, actress [Rose McGowan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rose_McGowan) was wearing a see-through dress, no bra, and a thong,[^cite_note-mcgowan-44] while [Mariah Carey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mariah_Carey) and [Whitney Houston](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Whitney_Houston) poked fun at their rumored rift by wearing lookalike chocolate brown dresses by [Vera Wang](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vera_Wang). In an attempt to outdo each other, the singers tore off pieces of their dresses to reveal minidresses then staged a faux [catfight](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catfight) that left the audience in disbelief. "People thought Whitney and I had some kind of beef", explained Carey.[^cite_note-carey-45]

**1999**: [Lil' Kim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lil'_Kim) showed up at the [1999 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1999_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) with an entire breast exposed and only a small pasty over the nipple. Kim's outfit became even more controversial later when she appeared on stage with [Diana Ross](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diana_Ross) and [Mary J. Blige](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mary_J._Blige) to present the Best Hip-Hop Video award, and Ross reached over, cupped her hand under Kim's exposed breast and jiggled it while Kim laughed.[^cite_note-careercrip-46]

During the following acceptance speech by the [Beastie Boys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beastie_Boys), group member [Ad-Rock](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ad-Rock) addressed the instances of rape and sexual assault that occurred in the crowd at the recent [Woodstock 1999](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Woodstock_1999) concert event. He pleaded to other musicians in the room to make a change in the way they treat fans at concerts; to pledge to talk with promoters and security to ensure "the safety of all the girls and the women who come to our shows".[^cite_note-npr-47]

[Afeni Shakur](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Afeni_Shakur) and Voletta Wallace, the mothers of the recently deceased [Tupac Shakur](31341.md) and [The Notorious B.I.G.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Notorious_B.I.G.), met for the first time at the ceremony.[^cite_note-vma1999-48]

[Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) made her debut appearance on the show, performing her single "[...Baby One More Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/...Baby_One_More_Time)" and later introducing [NSYNC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NSYNC) for "[Tearin' Up My Heart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tearin'_Up_My_Heart)".

[Ricky Martin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ricky_Martin) took the stage of the ceremony to perform his singles "[She's All I Ever Had](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/She's_All_I_Ever_Had)" and "[Livin' la Vida Loca](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Livin'_la_Vida_Loca)".[^cite_note-49]

[TLC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TLC_\(band\)) won Best Group Video for "[No Scrubs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/No_Scrubs)", receiving a [standing ovation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Standing_ovation) from the audience and artists. For the second year in a row, the [Backstreet Boys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Backstreet_Boys) took the Viewer's Choice Award for "[I Want It That Way](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I_Want_It_That_Way)", a song they performed during the broadcast.

Before presenting the final award, a group of [drag queens](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drag_queen) paid tribute to Madonna wearing her most iconic outfits with a medley of her hit songs. Madonna herself then appeared onstage and remarked, "All I have to say is that it takes a real man to fill my shoes." She then introduced [Paul McCartney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_McCartney), who presented the Video of the Year to "some guy called Laurence Hill" ([Lauryn Hill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lauryn_Hill)).

### 2000s

**2000**: at the [2000 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2000_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Limp Bizkit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Limp_Bizkit) won the award for Best Rock Video. As vocalist [Fred Durst](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fred_Durst) spoke, [Rage Against the Machine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rage_Against_the_Machine) bassist [Tim Commerford](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tim_Commerford) climbed onto the scaffolding of the set. Durst egged Commerford on, saying "Stage dive, dude" and "Take a dive", finally ending his speech with "and \[Commerford]'s a pussy 'cause he won't jump". Commerford and his [bodyguard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bodyguard) were sentenced to a night in jail. RATM vocalist [Zack de la Rocha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zack_de_la_Rocha) reportedly left the awards after Commerford's stunt. RATM guitarist [Tom Morello](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tom_Morello) recalled that Commerford related his plan to the rest of the band before the show, and that both de la Rocha and Morello advised him against it immediately after Bizkit was presented the award.[^cite_note-billboard1-50][^cite_note-limp-51]

[Eminem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eminem) performed his two singles "[The Real Slim Shady](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Real_Slim_Shady)" and "[The Way I Am](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Way_I_Am_\(Eminem_song\))". The performance began with Eminem's single "The Real Slim Shady" which started outside the [Radio City Music Hall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radio_City_Music_Hall) on [6th Avenue, Manhattan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sixth_Avenue). Eminem continued his performance into the arena and was followed by a hundred Eminem 'clones' wearing white tank tops and baggy jeans with bleached blonde hair.[^cite_note-52]

[Napster](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Napster) co-founder [Shawn Fanning](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shawn_Fanning) co-introduced [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears). Fanning wore a [Metallica](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metallica) T-shirt, much to the displeasure of Metallica drummer and anti-Napster advocate [Lars Ulrich](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lars_Ulrich). Britney performed "[(I Can't Get No) Satisfaction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/\(I_Can't_Get_No\)_Satisfaction)" and her newest smash hit "[Oops!... I Did It Again](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oops!..._I_Did_It_Again_\(song\))", which gained media attention mostly due to Spears's ripping off a tuxedo to reveal nude-colored performance attire. At performance's end, VMA co-host Marlon Wayans proclaimed, "Girl done went from '[The Mickey Mouse Club](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_All_New_Mickey_Mouse_Club)' to the strip club."[^cite_note-britvma-53]

At the end of [Christina Aguilera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christina_Aguilera)'s performance, Durst walked onstage and performed part of his band's song "Livin' It Up" with Aguilera. After eliciting charged reactions from his fans, Durst stated: "I already told you guys before, I did it all for the [nookie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nookie_\(Limp_Bizkit_song\)), man."[^cite_note-nookie1-54] The feud died weeks later. Aguilera denied Durst's statement, saying Durst "got no nookie".[^cite_note-nookie2-55]

Aguilera and Spears disproved rumors of a rivalry when they came onstage, holding hands, and introduced [Whitney Houston](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Whitney_Houston). Houston, who had been targeted by the media for erratic behavior, canceled appearances, drug use rumors and being busted for carrying [marijuana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marijuana) at a Hawaii airport just a few months prior, came out to a standing ovation and introduced an award to Eminem with husband [Bobby Brown](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bobby_Brown), who was recently let out from jail. Houston revealed more erratic behavior, jumping up and down saying "free" in reference to Brown's recent jail release. [Aaliyah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aaliyah) would win two MTV Video Music Awards for Best Female Video and Best Video from a Film for ["Try Again"](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Try_Again_\(Aaliyah_song\)).

**2001**: at the [2001 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2001_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) performed her single, "[I'm a Slave 4 U](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I'm_a_Slave_4_U)". while dancing in a very revealing outfit, the performance featured the singer in a cage with a [tiger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tiger) and briefly dancing with a real [albino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albino) snake on her shoulders. The inclusion of a tiger and a snake in the performance bought a great deal of criticism from [PETA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/People_for_the_Ethical_Treatment_of_Animals).[^cite_note-britvma-53][^cite_note-bcm-56]

[U2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U2) had been set to perform a medley including "[Elevation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elevation_\(song\))", "[Beautiful Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beautiful_Day)" and "[Stuck in a Moment You Can't Get Out Of](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stuck_in_a_Moment_You_Can't_Get_Out_Of)". The riser they were performing on lost power and MTV had to cut to several minutes of promos while everything was set up again. They lost power again and finally the band was lowered to the ground and performed without the riser. After the performance, lead singer [Bono](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bono) explained that MTV "forgot to pay the electricity bill".[^cite_note-vma2001-57]

[Michael Jackson](14995351.md) made a surprise appearance at the end of [NSYNC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NSYNC)'s performance of "[Pop](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pop_\(NSYNC_song\))". Surrounded by members of the group, Jackson performed some of his trademark dance moves to [Justin Timberlake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justin_Timberlake)'s [beatboxing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beatbox).[^cite_note-vma2001-57]

The show paid tribute to the [R\&B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Contemporary_R\&B) singer/actress [Aaliyah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aaliyah), who was supposed to present an award but [died](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2001_Marsh_Harbour_Cessna_402_crash) two weeks before in a plane crash. U2 paid tribute to [punk-rock](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Punk-rock) singer [Joey Ramone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joey_Ramone), who died of [lymphoma](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lymphoma) 4 months before.[^cite_note-58]

**2002**: in 2002, [the VMAs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2002_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) took place on [Michael Jackson](14995351.md)'s birthday. [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) introduced Jackson as the "artist of the millennium" and presented him with a birthday cake and a statue. Jackson, believing he won an honor, gave an acceptance speech. In his speech he said, "When I was a little boy growing up in Indiana if someone told me I'd be getting 'the artist of the millennium award,' I'd have never believed it."[^cite_note-59] MTV confirmed no such award existed, and the statue was merely a birthday gift. A year later, at the [2003 VMAs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2003_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), this was parodied by presenters [Fred Durst](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fred_Durst) and [Jack Black](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jack_Black), with Durst stating that Black was the "funniest man alive".[^cite_note-britvma-53][^cite_note-artistsofmill-60]

[The Hives](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Hives) and [The Vines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Vines_\(band\)) both performed on the night after the sudden [garage rock revival](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Garage_rock_revival) during the year, with rumors spreading around that it was to be 'battle of the bands' performance. Once The Hives performed, singer [Howlin' Pelle Almqvist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Howlin'_Pelle_Almqvist) said to the crowd "I know you want us to play more, but that's all the time we have for so you can turn off \[your TV] now",[^cite_note-61] just before The Vines came on the bigger stage to perform their hit single "[Get Free](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Get_Free_\(The_Vines_song\))", ultimately destroying their set after their performance.

[Triumph the Insult Comic Dog](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Triumph_the_Insult_Comic_Dog) appeared and had a confrontation with Eminem. Eminem had a confrontation with [Moby](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moby), who had called the rapper's music [misogynistic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Misogyny) and [homophobic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homophobia). By that time, there were multiple boos from the crowd. The Best Male Video award was given to Eminem right after the confrontation with Triumph and Moby, and when Eminem went to stage to pick up the award, in the middle of his speech, he challenged Moby to fight if he continued to boo him.[^cite_note-2002em-62]

[Justin Timberlake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justin_Timberlake) made his solo performance debut on the show, performing his single "[Like I Love You](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Like_I_Love_You)".

[Tionne "T-Boz" Watkins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tionne_%22T-Boz%22_Watkins) and [Rozonda "Chilli" Thomas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rozonda_%22Chilli%22_Thomas), the surviving members of the top-selling [R\&B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Contemporary_R\&B) trio [TLC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TLC_\(band\)), made their first television appearance since the death of bandmate [Lisa "Left Eye" Lopes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lisa_%22Left_Eye%22_Lopes).[^cite_note-2002em-62]

[Shakira](6479315.md) took the stage of the VMAs to perform her single "[Objection (Tango)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Objection_\(Tango\))".

[Christina Aguilera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christina_Aguilera) shocked the audience by wearing a skimpy outfit and heavy makeup, with a dirty look removing her good-girl image.[^cite_note-vma2002-63] She was part of an awkward moment at the awards when presenting the Best Male Video Award to Eminem, who had insulted her in 2000.[^cite_note-emxtina-64]

Canadian artist [Avril Lavigne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avril_Lavigne) won her first award ever as Best New Artist in a Video, getting the record for the youngest artist so far to win this award at the age of 17. She kicked off the 2002 VMAs with a "[Complicated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Complicated_\(Avril_Lavigne_song\))" / "[Sk8er Boi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sk8er_Boi)" pre-show performance and together with [Lisa Marie Presley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lisa_Marie_Presley) they gave the award for Best Female Video to [Pink](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pink_\(singer\)) who was under the effects of alcohol during her acceptance speech.[^cite_note-2002em-62]

[Axl Rose](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Axl_Rose) unveiled the new lineup of [New Guns N' Roses](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guns_N'_Roses). The band's set was the show's finale, and although the performance was meant to be kept a secret, some New York radio outlets announced the performance earlier in the day. The band played a set consisting of "[Welcome to the Jungle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Welcome_to_the_Jungle)", "[Madagascar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madagascar_\(song\))", and "[Paradise City](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paradise_City)".[^cite_note-gunsnroses-65]

**2003**: at the [2003 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2003_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) played a [groom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Groom) kissing her [brides](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bride), [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) and [Christina Aguilera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christina_Aguilera), on stage. The gender role-reversal and [lesbian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lesbian) theme instantly made front-page headlines. The three singers performed a [medley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medley_\(music\)) of her classic hit, "[Like a Virgin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Like_a_Virgin_\(song\))", and her then latest release, "[Hollywood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hollywood_\(Madonna_song\))", with a guest rap by [Missy Elliott](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Missy_Elliott). A quick camera cut to the reaction of [Justin Timberlake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justin_Timberlake), who dated Spears until 2002, also gained media attention.[^cite_note-66] The design resembled Madonna's performance of "Like a Virgin" at the [1984 VMAs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1984_MTV_Video_Music_Awards): the same wedding cake set, wedding dresses and "Boy Toy" belt worn by Madonna 19 years ago now adorned Aguilera and Spears.[^cite_note-autogenerated1-67]

[Beyoncé](83688.md) opened her performance by descending upside down from the [rafters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rafter) at Radio City Music Hall. Knowles performed "[Baby Boy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baby_Boy_\(Beyonc%C3%A9_song\))" as she was gently lowered to the stage, dressed in harem pants. She was later joined by [Jay-Z](162870.md) as they performed their duet "[Crazy in Love](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Crazy_in_Love)". The performance marked Knowles' solo debut.[^cite_note-beyonce-68]

**2004**: the [2004 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2004_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) were held at the [American Airlines Arena](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_Airlines_Arena) in [Miami, Florida](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miami,_Florida). This was the first time the awards show was held outside of New York City and Los Angeles. It was the first video music awards to not have a host.[^cite_note-2004host-69]

**2005**: at the [2005 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2005_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), [Green Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_Day) returned, taking home the Best Rock Video, Best Group Video, and Video of the Year Moonmen for "[Boulevard of Broken Dreams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boulevard_of_Broken_Dreams_\(Green_Day_song\))", which they performed during the broadcast. They also took the Viewer's Choice award for "[American Idiot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_Idiot_\(song\))".

The 2005 VMAs were the first to have a performance in [Spanish](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spanish_\(language\)), when [Shakira](6479315.md) performed her single "[La Tortura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Tortura)" with [Alejandro Sanz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alejandro_Sanz). It was also the first time that Spanish-language videos were nominated at the awards.[^cite_note-vma2005-70]

Also at the show, while introducing the [reggaeton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reggaeton) spot, [Fat Joe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fat_Joe) made a disparaging comment about [G-Unit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/G-Unit): "I'd like to tell the people home I feel so safe tonight with all this police protection courtesy of G-Unit...". Later in the show, after G-Unit's performance, [50 Cent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/50_Cent) directed profanities at Fat Joe which were edited out before the shows airing although some broadcasts played the uncensored version of the show.[^cite_note-fatjoe-71] Reggaeton artists [Daddy Yankee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daddy_Yankee), [Tego Calderón](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tego_Calder%C3%B3n) and [Don Omar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Don_Omar) performed.

Actress [Eva Longoria](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eva_Longoria) caused a stir when she appeared barely dressed to introduce [Mariah Carey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mariah_Carey).[^cite_note-eva-72] [Gwen Stefani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gwen_Stefani) and [Snoop Dogg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snoop_Dogg) won best dressed female and male and received prizes to donate to a charity of their choice.[^cite_note-bestdressed-73]

[Kelly Clarkson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kelly_Clarkson) performed a high energy version of her song "[Since U Been Gone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Since_U_Been_Gone)" barefoot wearing a graphic belly top and capri pants. Clarkson moved through the crowd during the performance, which culminated in the singer getting sprayed with water. By the end of the song, she and a portion of the audience were soaking wet.[^cite_note-kellyclarkson-74] [R. Kelly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/R._Kelly) performed his rap opera, "[Trapped in the Closet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trapped_in_the_Closet)".[^cite_note-vma2005-70]

Animated duo [Beavis and Butt-head](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beavis_and_Butt-head) appeared in several Viewer's Choice award skits, saying to "Vote to put Beavis and Butt-head back on MTV!".[^cite_note-press2005-75] Rather belatedly, the TV show returned to MTV in 2011.

**2006**: [Justin Timberlake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justin_Timberlake) opened the [2006 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) performing his singles "[My Love](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/My_Love_\(Justin_Timberlake_song\))" and "[SexyBack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SexyBack)". The awards were distributed evenly widespread all genres, as hip-hop, pop and rock artists alike all won Moonmen. 2006 was also the first time that viewers voted for all the performer's categories (Video of the Year, Best Male Video, Best Group Video, among others, except professional categories).[^cite_note-vma2006-76]

When [Panic! at the Disco](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Panic!_at_the_Disco) won Video of the Year for "[I Write Sins Not Tragedies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I_Write_Sins_Not_Tragedies)", a man calling himself Sixx jumped onstage claiming that MTV had denied him his own TV show.[^cite_note-vma2006-76] Hosted by [Jack Black](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jack_Black), there were also performances by [Shakira](6479315.md) with [Wyclef Jean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wyclef_Jean), [Beyoncé](83688.md), [Christina Aguilera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christina_Aguilera) and [The Killers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Killers).

**2007**: the [2007 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2007_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) was opened by [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) performing her comeback single "[Gimme More](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gimme_More)". To many, Spears failed to live up to the pre-show hype. She wore black sequined lingerie and put in less energy than her previous performances.[^cite_note-nydaily-77][^cite_note-people-78] The performance was called "career crippling".[^cite_note-careercrip-46]

[Kid Rock](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kid_Rock) and [Tommy Lee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tommy_Lee) were involved in an altercation during [Alicia Keys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alicia_Keys)'s performance. Lee was sitting with magician [Criss Angel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Criss_Angel) when the two went to visit [Diddy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sean_Combs), who was sitting close to Kid Rock. Kid Rock allegedly punched Tommy Lee in the face. The two were broken up, and both were escorted from the resort. Angel was removed a short time later.[^cite_note-fight-79]

**2008**: the [2008 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2008_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) were opened by [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) which welcomed everybody to the 25-year anniversary of the Video Music Awards. Barbadian singer [Rihanna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rihanna) opened the show with her song "[Disturbia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disturbia_\(song\))" in a very revealing black leather outfit. After 16 nominations, Britney Spears finally won her first VMA, taking Best Female Video, Best Pop Video, and Video of the Year for "[Piece of Me](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piece_of_Me_\(Britney_Spears_song\))", considered as Spears' comeback.

Host [Russell Brand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russell_Brand) commented on Democratic presidential nominee [Barack Obama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barack_Obama), begging the audience to vote for him. Brand called President [George W. Bush](3414021.md) a "retarded cowboy" and claimed Bush "wouldn't be trusted with a pair of scissors in Britain".[^cite_note-brand-80]

&#x20;Russell Brand also made comments on the [Jonas Brothers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jonas_Brothers) for wearing [purity rings](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Purity_rings). Brand later faced criticism for his swipes at the trio. He confirmed his apology at the [MTV Video Music Awards 2009](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV_Video_Music_Awards_2009) by saying "I upset the Jonas Brothers last year, I had to say sorry to them and they forgave me. They had to, they're Christians." During her introduction of T.I. and Rihanna's performance, [Jordin Sparks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jordin_Sparks), who also wears a purity ring, defended the boy-band by saying "It's not bad to wear a promise ring because not everybody, guy or girl, wants to be a slut." Sparks was in turn criticized for implying that those who do not wear purity rings or do not abstain are promiscuous.[^cite_note-jordin-81]



[Kanye West](523032.md) taking the microphone from [Taylor Swift](5422144.md) at the *[2009 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2009_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)*

**2009**: at the [2009 Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2009_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), singer-songwriter [Taylor Swift](5422144.md) won Best Female video for "[You Belong with Me](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/You_Belong_with_Me)". During her acceptance speech, rapper [Kanye West](523032.md) unexpectedly showed up on stage. Taking the microphone from Swift, he announced "Yo Taylor, I'm really happy for you, and Imma let you finish, but [Beyoncé](83688.md) had one of the best videos of all time. One of the best videos of all time!".[^cite_note-guardian-82][^cite_note-kanye-83] MTV cut away from the stage (showing Beyoncé with a shocked and disapproved look) after West gave the microphone back to Swift and giving the middle finger to the audience as they started booing him off stage, and so Swift did not finish her speech. West was removed for the rest of the show. However, when Beyoncé won Video of the Year for "[Single Ladies (Put a Ring on It)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Single_Ladies_\(Put_a_Ring_on_It\))", she called Swift back on stage to "have her moment" (finishing her speech from winning Best Female Video). In an off-the-record portion of an interview the following day, President [Barack Obama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barack_Obama) called West a "jackass" for his antics.[^cite_note-huffington-84] During a post-interview, Jay-Z admitted West was wrong to go on stage, but he thought people were overreacting to the incident.[^cite_note-telegraph-85]

[Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) opened the show talking about her experiences with [Michael Jackson](14995351.md). After her speech, a Michael Jackson tribute commenced, including [Janet Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Janet_Jackson) performing the duet "[Scream](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scream_\(Michael_Jackson_and_Janet_Jackson_song\))". The show closed with a sneak preview of Michael Jackson's *[This Is It](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson's_This_Is_It)* movie.[^cite_note-thisisit-86]

At the show's red carpet, Colombian singer [Shakira](6479315.md) and American singer [Pink](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pink_\(singer\)) attended with the same dress. Both singers took it humorously and posed together for the paparazzi.

[Lady Gaga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lady_Gaga) later performed her song "[Paparazzi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paparazzi_\(Lady_Gaga_song\))" and shocked the audience when she appeared to bleed out on stage. It was actually a contraption in her outfit.[^cite_note-87] [Beyoncé](83688.md) performed her song "[Single Ladies (Put a Ring on It)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Single_Ladies_\(Put_a_Ring_on_It\))" with over two dozen female dancers dancing with her.[^cite_note-vma2009-88] [Pink](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pink_\(singer\)) performed her single "[Sober](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sober_\(Pink_song\))" while doing a trapeze act during the entire performance while singing live.

Rapper [Lil Mama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lil_Mama) jumped on stage as [Jay-Z](162870.md) and [Alicia Keys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alicia_Keys) performed "[Empire State of Mind](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Empire_State_of_Mind)".[^cite_note-lilmama-89] Lil Mama later apologized.[^cite_note-hellobeautiful-90] During an interview with New York Radio DJ [Angie Martinez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angie_Martinez), Jay-Z said that he thought the unrehearsed move was something similar to the West and Swift incident.[^cite_note-hellobeautiful-90]

Beyoncé, Lady Gaga and [Green Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_Day) were the big winners of the night, with three awards each.[^cite_note-vma2009-88]

### 2010s



The standing inflatable KAWS moonman at the [2013 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)

**2010:** at the [2010 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2010_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), Taylor Swift performed her song "[Innocent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Innocent_\(Taylor_Swift_song\))", while Kanye West performed "Runaway" with [Pusha T](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pusha_T) giving a stellar performance on the [MPC2000](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MPC2000).[^cite_note-91] [Eminem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eminem), who performed at the show, did not receive his awards in person, as he had to leave immediately to perform with [Jay Z](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jay_Z) at [The Home & Home Tour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Home_&_Home_Tour) in New York City the next day.[^cite_note-92] Additionally, [will.i.am](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Will.i.am)'s "[blackface](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blackface)" outfit sparked controversy among African-Americans.[^cite_note-blackface-93] [Florence + the Machine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Florence_and_the_Machine) performed "[Dog Days Are Over](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dog_Days_Are_Over)".[^cite_note-94]

[Lady Gaga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lady_Gaga) won eight awards, including Video of the Year, Best Female Video, and Best Pop Video for "[Bad Romance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bad_Romance)"; she also won Best Collaboration for "[Telephone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Telephone_\(song\))" with [Beyoncé](83688.md). Upon accepting her Video of the Year award, Gaga wore a [dress made entirely of raw meat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lady_Gaga's_meat_dress), which drew criticism from [PETA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/People_for_the_Ethical_Treatment_of_Animals).[^cite_note-95] While accepting her Video of the Year award, Lady Gaga announced the title of her next album, *[Born This Way](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Born_This_Way_\(album\))*, and sang the chorus of its title track and lead single. [Justin Bieber](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justin_Bieber) won Best New Artist, making him the second youngest Canada-born artist to ever win a VMA (first being Avril Lavigne in 2002).[^cite_note-beiber-96]

Overall, the show grabbed 11.4 million viewers—the largest audience for an MTV Video Music Awards since [2002](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2002_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), and until [2011](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_MTV_Video_Music_Awards)'s new record.[^cite_note-largest-97]

**2011**: the [2011 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) returned to the [Nokia Theatre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microsoft_Theater) in Los Angeles, California. In the entire history of the show, this was the third time no host was scheduled, although [Kevin Hart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kevin_Hart_\(actor\)), who would receive the position a year later, delivered an opening monologue.

[Lady Gaga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lady_Gaga) opened the show dressed as her alter ego [Joe Calderone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joe_Calderone), delivering a monologue and then performing her single "[You and I](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/You_and_I_\(Lady_Gaga_song\))" from her album *[Born This Way](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Born_This_Way_\(album\))*. She was joined on stage by [Queen band](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Queen_band) member and guitarist [Brian May](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brian_May).

The tribute to [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) included performances by young talents, who danced to various hit singles by Spears including "[...Baby One More Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/...Baby_One_More_Time)" and "[Till the World Ends](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Till_the_World_Ends)". After the tribute, [Lady Gaga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lady_Gaga) went onstage to present Spears the [Michael Jackson Video Vanguard Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_Video_Vanguard_Award).[^cite_note-98] While accepting her award she and Gaga (dressed as her alter ego Joe Calderone) teased a kiss. Spears rejected the exchange saying, "I've done that already", referring to her liplock with Madonna at the 2003 show.[^cite_note-99]

During [Kanye West](523032.md) and [Jay Z](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jay_Z)'s surprise performance of "[Otis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Otis_\(song\))", a man attempted to rush the stage but was quickly taken off by security.[^cite_note-100]

[Katy Perry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Katy_Perry) and [Kanye West](523032.md) won the Award for Best Video for a Collaborative Single. As they went onstage to receive the award, Perry made a reference to West's incident with [Taylor Swift](5422144.md) two years back. This was the first award Perry won in four years of being nominated.[^cite_note-101]

Prior to her performance, [Beyoncé](83688.md) announced on the red carpet that she and husband/rapper [Jay Z](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jay_Z) were expecting a child. Straight after Beyoncé finished performing her song "[Love on Top](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Love_on_Top)", she also revealed again that she was pregnant by showing off her baby bump and rubbing it. That evening, Beyoncé set the record for the most mentions on [Twitter](9988187.md) per second (with 8,868) and helped this year's VMAs become the most-watched broadcast in MTV history, pulling in 12.4 million viewers.[^cite_note-102]

A tribute to late singer [Amy Winehouse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amy_Winehouse) also took place; [Russell Brand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russell_Brand) delivered a monologue on his thoughts on her, after which [Tony Bennett](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tony_Bennett) previewed his collaboration with her for his album *[Duets II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Duets_II_\(Tony_Bennett_album\))*. Afterwards, [Bruno Mars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bruno_Mars) performed "[Valerie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Valerie_\(The_Zutons_song\))" in her honor.[^cite_note-103]

Katy Perry won three awards including Video of the Year for "[Firework](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Firework_\(song\))", and Lady Gaga won two awards, including the new category Best Video With a Message for "[Born This Way](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Born_This_Way_\(song\))".[^cite_note-104]

**2012**: The [2012 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) aired live from the [Staples Center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Staples_Center) in Los Angeles, California on September 6, 2012. British boy band [One Direction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One_Direction) was the big winner of the night receiving Best New Artist, Best Pop Video, and Most Share Worthy Video. [Rihanna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rihanna) won the Video of the Year Award for "[We Found Love](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/We_Found_Love)".

**2013**: For the [2013 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_MTV_Video_Music_Awards), MTV redesigned its moonman statue[^cite_note-105] for one year only due to the ceremony's being held in [Brooklyn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brooklyn) for the first time. Brooklyn-based artist [KAWS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KAWS_\(artist\)) used [Michelin Man](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michelin_Man) as inspiration to redesign the image of the renowned [MTV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV) moonman. [Barclays Center](1658814.md), which served as the venue for the 2013 show, was decorated also by KAWS, themed around the redesigned moonman. The 2013 VMAs also marked the fourth time in the award show's history that a host had not been appointed for the annual event.[^cite_note-106] [Lady Gaga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lady_Gaga) opened the ceremony, performing her single "[Applause](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Applause_\(Lady_Gaga_song\))". During the performance, Gaga changed three times on stage and gave a tribute to her past eras (*[The Fame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Fame)*, *[The Fame Monster](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Fame_Monster)* and *[Born This Way](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Born_This_Way_\(album\))*). At the end, Gaga came on stage wearing nothing but a seashell bikini.

[Miley Cyrus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miley_Cyrus)' performance of her song "[We Can't Stop](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/We_Can't_Stop)" featured the former Disney star entering the stage through a giant teddy bear wearing a one-piece bodice with a teddy bear design on it. The singer performed with a group of female backup dancers dressed as giant bears before singer [Robin Thicke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robin_Thicke) joins with Cyrus on stage to perform his single "[Blurred Lines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blurred_Lines)". Cyrus then stripped down to a nude-colored lingerie and proceeded to perform her signature [twerking](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twerking) dance moves, simulating sex with Thicke and grabbing his crotch with a giant foam finger.[^cite_note-107] The unimpressed reactions of several celebrity audience members, including [Rihanna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rihanna) and members of [One Direction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One_Direction), also gained attention in the media.[^cite_note-108]

[Justin Timberlake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justin_Timberlake)'s "[Mirrors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mirrors_\(Justin_Timberlake_song\))" won Video of the Year, making him the first male solo artist to win this category since Eminem in 2002 with "Without Me". Timberlake also became the second artist to be honored with the [Video Vanguard Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_Video_Vanguard_Award) and win Video of the Year in the same night, after [Peter Gabriel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Gabriel) did it in 1987. To celebrate his Video Vanguard Award, Timberlake performed a 15-minute medley performance, which included a mini-reunion with his former band [\*NSYNC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/*NSYNC).[^cite_note-109] The set list of the most watched performance of the night[^cite_note-110] included hit singles from his first four solo albums, such as "[Cry Me a River](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cry_Me_a_River_\(Justin_Timberlake_song\))", "[SexyBack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SexyBack)" and "[Suit & Tie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suit_&_Tie)".

**2014**: the [2014 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) was held at [The Forum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Forum_\(Inglewood,_California\)) in [Inglewood, California](107640.md). [Miley Cyrus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miley_Cyrus) won Video of the Year for "[Wrecking Ball](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wrecking_Ball_\(Miley_Cyrus_song\))" and got a young homeless man named Jesse to accept the award on her behalf. His speech was a call-to-action to help raise awareness to the homeless youth of America.[^cite_note-111] [Ariana Grande](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ariana_Grande), [Nicki Minaj](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nicki_Minaj) and [Jessie J](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jessie_J) opened the show.[^cite_note-112] [Beyoncé](83688.md) closed the show by performing a 16-minute medley of songs from her [self-titled fifth studio album](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beyonc%C3%A9_\(album\)). After the performance, her husband [Jay Z](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jay_Z) and daughter Blue Ivy presented the [Video Vanguard Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jackson_Video_Vanguard_Award) to her.[^cite_note-113]

**2015**: the [2015 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) was hosted by [Miley Cyrus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miley_Cyrus). Fashion designer [Jeremy Scott](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeremy_Scott) redesigned the year's "moonman", marking the second time that the statue had been transformed.[^cite_note-114] [Taylor Swift](5422144.md) won four awards, including Video of the Year for "Bad Blood".[^cite_note-115] She presented the Video Vanguard Award to [Kanye West](523032.md), who ended his acceptance speech by claiming that [he would run for president in 2020](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kanye_West_2020_presidential_campaign).[^cite_note-116]

Nicki Minaj ended her acceptance speech for [Best Hip-Hop Video](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV_Video_Music_Award_for_Best_Hip-Hop_Video) by passing the show back to Cyrus, saying: "And now, back to this bitch who had a lot to say about me the other day in the press: Miley, What's good?".[^cite_note-blast-117] Minaj was referring to Cyrus' comments about how she handled "[Anaconda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anaconda_\(Nicki_Minaj_song\))" being snubbed for a Video of the Year nomination. Cyrus appeared stunned by Minaj's remarks and replied, "We're all in this industry. We all do interviews and we all know how they manipulate shit. Nicki, congratu-fuckin-lations."[^cite_note-blast-117] The camera then cut back to Minaj who seemed to mouth back the word "bitch".[^cite_note-blast-117] Cyrus closed the show by performing along with a group of [drag queens who participated on *RuPaul's Drag Race*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_RuPaul's_Drag_Race_contestants) and surprisingly released her fifth studio album *[Miley Cyrus and Her Dead Petz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miley_Cyrus_and_Her_Dead_Petz)* for free.[^cite_note-118]

**2016**: the [2016 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) was held at the [Madison Square Garden](75129.md) in [Manhattan](45470.md). [Rihanna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rihanna) performed four times during the show, including opening and closing the ceremony. After her final performance, Rihanna was presented the Video Vanguard Award by [Drake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drake_\(musician\)), who declared, "She's someone I've been in love with since I was 22 years old."[^cite_note-119] While accepting her award, Drake leaned in for a kiss that Rihanna dodged.[^cite_note-demolished-120] [Britney Spears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Britney_Spears) returned to perform at the VMAs for the first time since her heavily criticized performance at the 2007 show.[^cite_note-demolished-120] [Kanye West](523032.md) gave a seven-minute long speech covering different topics, including his feud with [Taylor Swift](5422144.md).[^cite_note-121]

[Beyoncé](83688.md) performed a 16-minute medley of her album *[Lemonade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lemonade_\(album\))* and won eight awards, including Video of the Year for "[Formation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Formation_\(song\))".[^cite_note-demolished-120][^cite_note-passes-122] She became the most awarded artist in VMA history with 24 moonmen, surpassing Madonna's previous record of 20.[^cite_note-passes-122] The telecast saw a drop in audience for third year in a row.[^cite_note-123]

**2017**: the [2017 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) was hosted by [Katy Perry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Katy_Perry). [Kendrick Lamar](29909823.md) won six awards, including Video of the Year for "[Humble](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Humble_\(song\))", becoming the first artist to have won the award for a video he co-directed, while [Pink](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pink_\(singer\)) was honored with the Video Vanguard Award.[^cite_note-2017_vmas-124] [Jared Leto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jared_Leto) paid tribute to [Chester Bennington](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chester_Bennington) and [Chris Cornell](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chris_Cornell), who both died over the past three months.[^cite_note-125] The music video for [Taylor Swift](5422144.md)'s song "[Look What You Made Me Do](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Look_What_You_Made_Me_Do)" premiered during the broadcast.[^cite_note-2017_vmas-124] Compared to the previous year, viewership was down from 6.5 million to 5.4 million viewers, making it the lowest viewed VMAs since [Nielsen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nielsen_ratings) started measuring the show in 1994.[^cite_note-126]

At the start of [Fifth Harmony](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fifth_Harmony)'s performance there were five silhouettes; one of them fell off the stage, referencing former group member [Camila Cabello](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Camila_Cabello) leaving the group the previous year.[^cite_note-127]

[Logic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Logic_\(rapper\)) performed his song "[1-800-273-8255](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1-800-273-8255_\(song\))" along with [Alessia Cara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alessia_Cara) and [Khalid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khalid_\(American_singer\)). Their stage was outlined with survivors of suicide loss and attempts, who wore T-shirts that had the phone number on the front and the phrase "You Are Not Alone" written on the back. During their performance, Logic gave a speech about thanking the audience for giving him a platform to talk about the issues "that mainstream media doesn't want to talk about: mental health, anxiety, suicide, depression".[^cite_note-logic-128] He further added, "I don't give a damn if you're black, white, or any color in between. I don't care if you're Christian, you're Muslim, you're gay, you're straight – I am here to fight for your equality, because I believe that we are all born equal, but we are not treated equally and that is why we must fight."[^cite_note-logic-128] Following the performance, the [National Suicide Prevention Lifeline](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Suicide_Prevention_Lifeline) experienced a 50% increase in calls.[^cite_note-logic-128]

**2018**: [Cardi B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cardi_B), the most nominated artist of the night, opened the [2018 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2018_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) with a brief speech, holding a VMA like a baby.[^cite_note-129] While receiving one of her awards, Cardi B stated, "all the love.. it's genuine, it's beautiful, and that's something that God give me that you can't buy. B-tch."[^cite_note-130] [Camila Cabello](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Camila_Cabello) won Video of the Year for "[Havana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Havana_\(Camila_Cabello_song\))" and [Jennifer Lopez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jennifer_Lopez) received the Vanguard Award.[^cite_note-131] With [Maluma](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maluma_\(singer\)) among the performers as well, it marked the VMAs with the most Latin music artists present at the ceremony since the 2005 show.[^cite_note-132]

Before introducing Cabello's category, [Madonna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madonna_\(entertainer\)) gave a tribute to the late [Aretha Franklin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aretha_Franklin). Most of the speech said the early origins of Madonna's career and was only tangentially Franklin-related,[^cite_note-133] which made critics describe the "bizarre" tribute as a "lengthy" anecdote about herself that involved Franklin's music.[^cite_note-134][^cite_note-135][^cite_note-136] Afterwards, Madonna explained that she was there to give the Video of the Year award as asked by MTV and to speak about some experience in her career with some connection with Aretha, not to make any kind of tribute.[^cite_note-137]

The viewership of the 2018 MTV Video Music Awards was down 9% overall and 15% in the important 18- to 49-year-old demographic group. The total viewership was 4.8 million.[^cite_note-2018_ratings-138]

**2019**: the [2019 show](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) was held at the [Prudential Center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prudential_Center) in [Newark, New Jersey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Newark,_New_Jersey). This was the first time the VMAs took place at the venue and in the state of [New Jersey](21648.md) (it became the fifth state to host the show after [New York](8210131.md), [California](5407.md), [Florida](18933066.md) and [Nevada](21216.md)).

The show was hosted by [Sebastian Maniscalco](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sebastian_Maniscalco). [Taylor Swift](5422144.md) won three awards, including Video of the Year for "[You Need to Calm Down](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/You_Need_to_Calm_Down)".[^cite_note-139] She became the second artist and first female artist to have won the award for a video she co-directed, and the fourth artist to win the category twice overall.[^cite_note-140] [Lil Nas X](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lil_Nas_X) and [Billy Ray Cyrus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Billy_Ray_Cyrus)'s "[Old Town Road](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Town_Road)" won two awards, including Song of the Year.[^cite_note-141] [Missy Elliott](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Missy_Elliott) received the Vanguard Award introduced by [Cardi B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cardi_B) and performed a medley. The performance was heavily choreographed and the set pieces alluded to her music videos "[The Rain (Supa Dupa Fly)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Rain_\(Supa_Dupa_Fly\))", "[Get Ur Freak On](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Get_Ur_Freak_On)", and "[Work It](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Work_It_\(Missy_Elliott_song\))".[^cite_note-auto-142][^cite_note-143] [Shawn Mendes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shawn_Mendes) & [Camila Cabello](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Camila_Cabello) performed their hit single "[Señorita](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Se%C3%B1orita_\(Shawn_Mendes_and_Camila_Cabello_song\))" for the first time ever in a performance characterized for its kissing tension. [Lizzo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lizzo) performed a set featuring her singles, "[Truth Hurts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Truth_Hurts_\(song\))" and "[Good as Hell](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Good_as_Hell)". During the performance she gave a short self-empowering monologue, and featured a figurative shadow of an oversized CGI rear end.[^cite_note-auto-142][^cite_note-144] [Normani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Normani) gave a performance of her song "[Motivation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Motivation_\(Normani_song\))",[^cite_note-145][^cite_note-146] that featured her doing intense gymnastic styled choreography that echoed the music video.[^cite_note-147][^cite_note-148][^cite_note-149] [Rosalía](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rosal%C3%ADa_\(singer\)) became the first lead Spanish act to ever perform at the VMAs, performing a medley of "[A Ningún Hombre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/A_ning%C3%BAn_hombre)", "[Yo x Ti, Tu x Mi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yo_x_Ti,_Tu_x_Mi)" (alongside [Ozuna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ozuna_\(singer\))), and "[Aute Cuture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aute_Cuture)".[^cite_note-150][^cite_note-151] She also won [Best Latin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV_Video_Music_Award_for_Best_Latin) and [Best Choreography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MTV_Video_Music_Award_for_Best_Choreography) for "[Con Altura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Con_Altura)", becoming the first ever Spanish act to win a VMA, for which she was congratulated by many personalities including [Prime Minister](203556.md) [Pedro Sánchez](43297512.md).[^cite_note-152]

[Marc Jacobs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marc_Jacobs) received the inaugural MTV Fashion Trailblazer Award.[^cite_note-153]

### 2020s

**2020**: The [2020 MTV Video Music Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020_MTV_Video_Music_Awards) were held on August 30, 2020.
